# Hesiodi Carmina, recensuit Aloisius Rzach (Teubner text: Theogony, Works and Days, Shield and the fragments, Greek with Latin prolegomena and testimonia)

- **Title:** Hesiodi Carmina, recensuit Aloisius Rzach (Teubner text: Theogony, Works and Days, Shield and the fragments, Greek with Latin prolegomena and testimonia)
- **Author:** Hesiod
- **Edition:** Leipzig: B. G. Teubner, 1908 (the small Teubner edition of Aloys Rzach, 1850-1935; Internet Archive item hesiodicarmina00hesi, Getty Research Institute copy, v + 263 pages). Greek text of the three poems with a critical apparatus, then the fragments with their testimonia; prolegomena in Latin.
- **Translator:** none (original Greek; Latin prolegomena by Rzach)
- **Year:** 1908
- **Read from:** https://archive.org/download/hesiodicarmina00hesi/hesiodicarmina00hesi_djvu.txt
- **Licence / copyright status:** Public domain (published 1908; Rzach died 1935, so more than 70 years; public domain in the United States by age).
---
